|\n\n[Back to top](https://www.seat61.com/train-travel-in-italy.htm#top)\n\n* * *\n\n## European Rail Timetable & maps\n\n[![Thomas Cook European Timetable -  click to buy online](https://www.seat61.com/images/ThomasCookEuropeanTT.gif)](http://www.europeanrailtimetable.eu/)![](https://www.seat61.com/images/white-spacer-20x140.gif)[![Traveller's Railway Map of Europe - buy online](https://www.seat61.com/images2/Europe-train-map.jpg)](https://www.amazon.co.uk/exec/obidos/ASIN/1838408002/themaninseats-21)![](https://www.seat61.com/images/white-spacer-20x140.gif)**The**\n**European Rail Timetable**\n(formerly the Thomas Cook European Timetable)\nhas train & ferry times for every country in Europe plus\ncurrency & climate information.  It is **essential**\nfor regular European train travellers and an inspiration for armchair\ntravellers.  Published since 1873, it had just celebrated 140 years of\npublication when Thomas Cook decided to pull the plug on their entire publishing\ndepartment, but the dedicated ex-Thomas Cook team set up a private venture and\nresumed publication of the famous European Rail Timetable in March 2014.\nYou can buy it online at\n[www.amazon.co.uk](https://www.amazon.co.uk/gp/search?ie=UTF8&keywords=European%20Rail%20Timetable&tag=themaninseats-21&index=books) (UK addresses) or\n[www.europeanrailtimetable.eu](https://www.europeanrailtimetable.eu/) (shipping worldwide).\n[More information \\\\\non what the European Rail Timetable contains](https://www.seat61.com/Thomas-Cook-European-Timetable.htm).\n\n**Rail Map Europe** is the map\nI recommend, covering all of Europe from\nPortugal in the west to Moscow & Istanbul in the east, Finland in the north\nto Sicily & Athens in the south.  Scenic routes & high-speed lines\nare highlighted.  [See an extract from \\\\\nthe map](https://www.seat61.com/include-files/pop-up-TCmap.htm).  Buy online at\n[www.europeanrailtimetable.eu](https://www.europeanrailtimetable.eu/) (shipping worldwide) or at\n[www.amazon.co.uk](https://www.amazon.co.uk/gp/product/https:/www.amazon.co.uk/gp/product/1838408002?ie=UTF8&tag=themaninseats-21)\n(UK addresses).\n\n[Back to top](https://www.seat61.com/train-travel-in-italy.htm#top)\n\n* * *\n\n## Suggested hotels in Italy\n\n**Here are my suggested hotels in key Italian cities, conveniently located for arrival by train**\n**and all with**\n**good or great reviews.  You are unlikely to be disappointed by any hotel**\n**scoring over 8.0 out of 10 on [Booking.com](https://www.booking.com/index.html?aid=339247).**\n\n**### Hotels in Turin**\n\n**- If you're only stopping overnight en route between London/Paris and**\n**Venice/Florence/Rome/Naples, the Paris-Milan trains serve**\n**[Turin Porta Susa station](https://www.seat61.com/stations/turin.htm) so you**\n**want a hotel nearby.**\n\n\n**Hotels near**\n**[Porta Susa station](https://www.seat61.com/stations/turin.htm) with good or**\n**great reviews include the**\n**[Hotel Torino Porta Susa](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/torino-porta-susa.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[Hotel Diplomatic](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/hoteldiplomatic.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[Best Quality Hotel Dock Milano](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/dock-milano.html?aid=339247) (across the road from the now-defunct**\n**original Porta Susa station building, immediately north of the new one), or the**\n**small but highly-recommended**\n**[Al Porta Susa B&B](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/al-porta-susa-b-b.en-gb.html?aid=339247).**\n\n\n**- If you're stopping for longer to**\n**visit Turin - and you should, it's one of Italy's most under-rated cities - I'd**\n**choose a hotel near [Turin Porta Nuova](https://www.seat61.com/stations/turin.htm#Porta_Nuova) as it's more central and most high-speed trains to Venice, Florence,**\n**Rome or Naples start here.**\n\n**Look no**\n**further than the lovely**\n**[Turin Palace Hotel](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/turin-palace.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**my**\n**favourite  hotel in Turin,**\n**just across the road from**\n**[Turin Porta Nuova](https://www.seat61.com/stations/turin.htm#Porta_Nuova)**\n**and walking distance from all the sights.  Friendly staff, lovely room,**\n**great breakfast, and it even has a roof terrace and bar - you won't be**\n**disappointed!**\n\n**An excellent**\n**alternative is the**\n**[Starhotels Majestic Hotel](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/hotel-majestic.en-gb.html?aid=339247), 200m from Porta Nuova station.**\n\n\n**### Hotels in Milan**\n\n**- There are lots of hotels near**\n**[Milan Centrale](https://www.seat61.com/stations/milan-centrale.htm), used**\n**by the Frecciarossa to/from Paris, the EuroCity trains to/from Switzerland and**\n**the majority of Italian domestic trains.**\n\n**Affordable hotels & guesthouses with good or great reviews just outside**\n**the station include the**\n**[Hotel Bristol](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/bristol-milano.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[Hotel Bernina](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/hotelbernina.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[43 Station Hotel](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/43-station.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[B&B Hotel Milano Central Station](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/b-amp-b-milano-central-station.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[Guesthouse Teodora](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/teodora-b-amp-b.en-gb.html?aid=339247).**\n\n**More upmarket hotels include**\n**[HD8 Hotel](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/hd8-milano.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[Glam Hotel](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/glam-milano.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[Made to Measure Business](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/made-to-measure-business.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[Starhotel Echo](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/starhotels-echo.en-gb.html?aid=339247) or**\n**[Starhotel Anderson](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/andersonhotel.en-gb.html?aid=339247).**\n\n**At the top end, the 5-star**\n**[Excelsior Hotel Gallia](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/excelsior-hotel-gallia.en-gb.html?aid=339247)**\n**is just across the road, you won't go wrong with that if your budget will**\n**stretch!**\n\n\n**- Near**\n**[Milan Porta Garibaldi](https://www.seat61.com/stations/milan-porta-garibaldi.htm)**\n**(used by the TGV to/from Paris), the**\n**[AC Milano Hotel (a Marriott Lifestyle Hotel)](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/ac-milano.en-gb.html?aid=339247) is**\n**just 350m from the station and gets good reviews.  Also try the**\n**[Holiday Inn Porta Garibaldi](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/milan-garibaldi-station.en-gb.html?aid=339247), which offers family rooms.**\n\n\n**### Hotels in Verona**\n\n**- Hotels near**\n**[Verona Porta Nuova \\**\\\n**station](https://www.seat61.com/stations/verona-porta-nuova.htm) with good or great reviews include the**\n**[Corte Merighi Rooms & Breakfast](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/corte-merighi.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[Novo Hotel Rossi](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/novohotelrossi.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[Guesthouse Verona](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/guesthouse-verona.en-gb.html?aid=339247) or (a little closer to the centre, but with fab**\n**reviews) the**\n**[Relais Empire](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/relais-empire-verona1.en-gb.html?aid=339247).**\n\n\n**### Hotels in Venice**\n\n**- _Location, location, location!_ Some people try to save money by staying on the mainland at**\n**[Mestre](https://www.seat61.com/stations/venice-santa-lucia.htm#venice-mestre)**\n**as**\n**hotels**\n**in Mestre are naturally cheaper.  It's an option, but don't do it if you**\n**don't have to.  You want to be a resident in Venice, not a serial day-tripper, so**\n**try to find an affordable place in the historic city of Venice itself.**\n\n**- [Venice Santa Lucia station](https://www.seat61.com/stations/venice-santa-lucia.htm) is walking distance**\n**from everywhere in central Venice including the Rialto Bridge and Piazza San Marco, so a hotel near the**\n**station which you can easily walk to with your bags makes a lot of sense.**\n\n**Relatively inexpensive places with**\n**good reviews near [Venice Santa Lucia](https://www.seat61.com/stations/venice-santa-lucia.htm) include**\n**[Albergo Marin](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/albergo-marin.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[Hotel Marte](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/marte.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[La Loggia della Luna](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/la-loggia-della-luna.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[Albergo ai Tolentini](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/albergo-ai-tolentini.en-gb.html?aid=339247).**\n**I personally recommend the quiet and friendly**\n**[Albergo Marin](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/albergo-marin.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**a short walk from [Venice Santa Lucia](https://www.seat61.com/stations/venice-santa-lucia.htm),**\n**I hadn't expected such an inexpensive hotel to offer egg & bacon for breakfast**\n**with a glass of Prosecco!**\n\n**For an upmarket place to stay I can**\n**personally recommend the luxurious and intimate**\n**[Hotel Canal Grande](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/canal-grande.en-gb.html?aid=339247), just 5**\n**minutes walk from [Venice Santa Lucia](https://www.seat61.com/stations/venice-santa-lucia.htm),**\n**I've stayed here with my son.**\n**Also try the**\n**[Abazzia De Luxe](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/abbazia-de-luxe.en-gb.html?aid=339247).**\n\n**For the least expensive**\n**accommodation in**\n**central Venice check**\n**[www.hostelworld.com](https://prf.hn/click/camref:1011lIIm/creativeref:1011l8373).**\n**Use the map view to see places in Venice itself.**\n\n**Of course, if you have the financial backing for a**\n**water taxi, then fine, stay near San Marco or wherever you like.  My son**\n**nagged me continually until I caved in and splurged €70 on a water taxi from**\n**Santa Lucia station to St Marks.  I have to say that it was the best thing**\n**I have ever done in Venice, and that includes a gondola ride!**\n\n\n**- If you _do_ decide to stay in Mestre**\n**to save money and travel in & out of Venice by train every day, try the**\n**[Hotel Cris](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/hotelcris.en-gb.html?aid=339247) which has great reviews 200m from**\n**[Venezia Mestre station](https://www.seat61.com/stations/venice-santa-lucia.htm#venice-mestre),**\n**and the**\n**[Best Western Plus Hotel Bologna](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/bestwestern.en-gb.html?aid=339247).**\n\n\n**### Hotels in Florence**\n\n**- For something fancy, try the**\n**[Hotel Santa Maria Novella](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/hotelsantamarianovella.en-gb.html?aid=339247) with a 9/10 review score and rooftop terrace overlooking**\n**the Basilica of Santa Maria Novella.  It's a mere 5-minute 400m walk from**\n**[Florence \\**\\\n**Santa Maria Novella station](https://www.seat61.com/stations/florence-santa-maria-novella.htm).**\n\n**- For something more affordable but**\n**also an easy walk from the station, try the**\n**[Hotel Art Atelier](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/art-atelier.en-gb.html?aid=339247).**\n\n**- Other hotels nearby with good**\n**reviews include**\n**[C-hotels Club](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/hotelclubfirenze.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[7Florence B&B](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/7florence.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[Hotel Lombardia](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/lombardia.en-gb.html?aid=339247).**\n\n\n**### Hotels in Rome**\n\n**- There are many hotels near**\n**[Rome Termini](https://www.seat61.com/stations/rome-termini.htm).  However, an affordable top choice with a 9/10 review rating**\n**is the**\n**[Hotel Diocleziano](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/diocleziano.en-gb.html?aid=339247), a 5-minute 400m walk from Roma Termini's main entrance,**\n**set in a 19th century building next to the Terme di Diocleziano ancient Roman**\n**baths.**\n\n**- An upmarket option near the station**\n**is**\n**[Hotel Le Petit](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/hotellepetitroma.en-gb.html?aid=339247), an 8-minute 700m walk from the station, also with great**\n**reviews.**\n\n**- Other hotels near the station with good or great**\n**reviews include**\n**[The Hive Hotel](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/the-hive.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[Dream Station](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/dream-statioon.en-gb.html?aid=339247),**\n**[The](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/smoothhotelrome.en-gb.html?aid=339247) [Republic Hotel](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/smoothhotelrome.en-gb.html?aid=339247).**\n\n\n**### Hotels in Naples**\n\n**- There are lots of hotels near**\n**[Naples Centrale](https://www.seat61.com/stations/naples-centrale.htm).**\n\n**- [The Starhotel Terminus](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/terminus.en-gb.html?aid=339247) is just across the road from the station, gets great**\n**reviews and has a roof terrace with views over the Bay of Naples.**\n\n**- [The Hotel Potenza](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/potenzanapoli.en-gb.html?aid=339247) is only a little further into the Piazza Garibaldi,**\n**cheaper, also with good reviews.**\n\n**- [The Unahotel Napoli](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/unahotel-napoli.en-gb.html?aid=339247) is highly recommended, on Piazza Mancini overlooking**\n**Piazza Garibaldi, on the far side facing the station.**\n\n**- Also consider the**\n**[Pit Stop Napoli Centrale](https://www.booking.com/hotel/it/napoli-garibaldi-luxury.en-gb.html?aid=339247).**\n\n\n**### [![Find hotels at Booking.com](https://www.seat61.com/images2/bookingdotcom1.jpg)Booking.com](https://www.booking.com/index.html?aid=339247)  for hotels**\n\n**I generally use [Booking.com](https://www.booking.com/index.html?aid=339247)**\n**for hotels for 3 reasons:**\n\n**(1) It keeps all my hotel bookings together in one place;**\n\n**(2) I've come to trust [Booking.com](https://www.booking.com/index.html?aid=339247)'s**\n**review scores;**\n\n**(3) [Booking.com](https://www.booking.com/index.html?aid=339247)**\n**usually offers a clearly-marked _Free cancellation_ option.**\n\n**Free**\n**cancellation means you can secure hotels risk-free even before trains open for**\n**booking, and if necessary change those bookings if your plans evolve.**\n\n**If I'm only staying a night or two, I look for a hotel near the**\n**station to make arrival & departure easy.  You can enter the station name (e.g.**\n**_Berlin Hbf_) as search location.  If staying longer, I look for a**\n**hotel close to the sights, entering the name of a city attraction as the search**\n**location, then using map view.**\n\n**I then look for a hotel with a review score of 8.0 or over, any hotel scoring**\n**over that won't disappoint.**\n\n**### AirBnB:  [Airbnb.com](https://airbnb.pvxt.net/c/1434491/567379/4273)**\n\n**[www.airbnb.com](https://airbnb.pvxt.net/c/1434491/567379/4273) began in 2008 when two designers who had space to share**\n**hosted three travellers looking for a place to stay.  AirBnB is a platform**\n**which connects hosts with guests, so you can now book a room in people's homes,**\n**or an apartment, flat or house which people want to rent out.  It can be**\n**nicer than a hostel, cheaper than many hotels.**\n\n**### [![](https://www.seat61.com/images/Hostelbookers_logo_200.gif)](https://prf.hn/click/camref:1011lIIm/creativeref:1011l8373)Backpacker hostels:  [Hostelworld.com](https://prf.hn/click/camref:1011lIIm/creativeref:1011l8373)**\n\n**[www.hostelworld.com](https://prf.hn/click/camref:1011lIIm/creativeref:1011l8373) offers online booking of**\n**dorm beds or cheap private rooms in backpacker hostels most European**\n**cities at rock-bottom prices.  It's one way to cut costs significantly**\n**compared to using a hotel every night.**\n\n[Back to top](https://www.seat61.com/train-travel-in-italy.htm#top)\n\n* * *\n\n## Travel insurance & other tips\n\n|     |     |\n| --- | --- |\n|  | [![Staysure travel insurance](https://www.seat61.com/images/staysure-insurance.jpg)](https://clk.tradedoubler.com/click?p=359210&a=1104089) |\n|  | [![Columbus Direct logo](https://www.seat61.com/images2/Columbus-direct.jpg)](https://www.tagserve.com/clickServlet?AID=204&MID=1&PID=1&SID=298&CID=1&SUBID=) |\n\n### Always take out travel insurance\n\nYou should have travel insurance with at least £1m or preferably £5m medical cover\nfrom a reliable insurer. It should cover trip cancellation and\nloss of cash & belongings up to a reasonable limit. An annual\npolicy is usually cheapest even for just 2 or 3 trips\na year, I have an annual policy with\n[Staysure.co.uk](https://clk.tradedoubler.com/click?p=359210&a=1104089)\nmyself.\nJust don't expect travel insurance to bail you out of every missed\nconnection, [see \\\\\nthe advice on missed connections here](https://www.seat61.com/european-train-travel.htm#when-things-go-wrong).  Here are some suggested\ninsurers, I get a little commission if you buy through these links,\n[feedback](https://www.seat61.com/email.htm) always welcome.\n\n![UK flag](https://www.seat61.com/images/Flag-UK.jpg)[www.staysure.co.uk](https://clk.tradedoubler.com/click?p=359210&a=1104089)\noffers enhanced Covid-19 protection and gets 4.7 out of 5 on\n[Trustpilot](https://uk.trustpilot.com/review/www.staysure.co.uk).\n\n![UK flag](https://www.seat61.com/images/Flag-UK.jpg)[www.columbusdirect.com](https://www.tagserve.com/clickServlet?AID=204&MID=1&PID=1&SID=298&CID=1&SUBID=) is also a well-know brand.\n\n![UK flag](https://www.seat61.com/images/Flag-Ireland.jpg)[www.staysure.com/ie](https://clk.tradedoubler.com/click?p=393165&a=1723688) if you live in Ireland.\n\n![US flag](https://www.seat61.com/images/Flag-USA.jpg)\nIf you live in the USA try\n[Travel Guard USA](http://www.jdoqocy.com/email-1489155-10400236).\n\n|     |     |\n| --- | --- |\n|  | [![Maya.net logo](https://www.seat61.com/images/maya-net-logo.jpg)](https://mayamobile.pxf.io/9VzQ0Q) |\n\n### [Get an eSIM](https://mayamobile.pxf.io/9VzQ0Q)  with mobile data package\n\nDon't rely on WiFi, download an eSIM with a European mobile data package and\nstay connected.  Most newer mobile phones can download a virtual SIM\nincluding iPhone 11 & later,\n[see device compatibility list](https://mayamobile.pxf.io/9VzQ0Q).\nThere's no need to buy a physical SIM card!\n[Maya.net](https://mayamobile.pxf.io/9VzQ0Q)\nis a reliable eSIM data retailer with a\n[4.5 out of \\\\\n5 Trustpilot rating](https://www.trustpilot.com/review/maya.net) and a\n[range of \\\\\npackages including unlimited data](https://mayamobile.pxf.io/9VzQ0Q).\n\n|     |     |\n| --- | --- |\n|  | [![Express VPN](https://www.seat61.com/images2/express-vpn.jpg)](https://www.seat61.com/booking/express-vpn.htm) |\n\n### [Get a VPN](https://www.seat61.com/booking/express-vpn.htm) for safe browsing.   _[Why you need a VPN](https://www.seat61.com/websites/vpn.htm)_\n\nWhen travelling you may use free public WiFi which is often insecure.  A VPN encrypts your connection so it's always\nsecure, even on unsecured WiFi.  It also means you can select the geographic location of the IP address you browse\nwith, to get around geoblocking which a surprising number of websites apply.\n[See VPNs & why you need one explained](https://www.seat61.com/websites/vpn.htm).\n[ExpressVPN](https://www.seat61.com/booking/express-vpn.htm)\nis a best buy with a\n[4.7 \\\\\nout of 5 Trustpilot ranking](https://uk.trustpilot.com/review/expressvpn.com) which I use myself - I've signed up as an ExpressVPN affiliate,\nand if you go with\n[expressvpn.com \\\\\nusing this link](https://www.seat61.com/booking/express-vpn.htm) you should see a special deal,\n3 months free with an annual subscription.  I also get some commission to help\nsupport this site.\n\n|     |     |\n| --- | --- |\n|  | [![Curve card](https://www.seat61.com/images2/curve-card.jpg)](http://curvecard.sjv.io/c/146261/1212032/12851)<br>[![Curve card](https://www.seat61.com/images2/curve-card2.jpg)](http://curvecard.sjv.io/c/146261/1212032/12851) |\n\n### [Get a Curve card](http://curvecard.sjv.io/c/146261/1212032/12851)  for foreign travel\n\nMost banks give you a poor\nexchange rate then add a foreign transaction fee on top.  A Curve\nMasterCard means no foreign transaction fees and gives you the mid-market\nexchange rate, at least up to a certain limit, £500 per month as I write this.\nThe money you spend on your Curve card goes straight onto one of your existing debit or\ncredit cards.  And you can get a Curve card for free.\n\n**How it works:**  1.\n[Download the \\\\\nCurve app for \\\\\niPhone or Android](http://curvecard.sjv.io/c/146261/1212032/12851).  2\\. Enter your details & they'll send you a Curve\nMasterCard - they send to the UK and most European addresses.  3.\nLink your existing credit & debit cards to the app, you can link up to two\ncards with the free version of Curve, I link my normal debit card and my normal\ncredit card.  4. Now use the Curve\nMasterCard to buy things online or in person or take cash from ATMs, exactly like a\nnormal MasterCard. Curve does the currency conversion and puts the balance in\nyour own currency onto\nwhichever debit or credit card is currently selected in the Curve app.  You can even change\nyour mind about which card it goes onto, within 14 days of the transaction.\n\nI have a Curve Blue card myself,\nit means I can buy a coffee on a foreign station on a card without being stung\nby fees and lousy exchange rates, just by tapping the Curve card on their card\nreader.  The money goes through Curve to my normal debit card and is taken\ndirectly from my account (in fact I have the Curve card set up as payment card\non Apple Pay on my iPhone, so can double-click my phone, let it do Face ID then\ntap the reader with the phone - even easier than getting a card out).  I get a little commission if you sign up to Curve, but I recommend it\nhere because I think it's great.\n[See details, download \\\\\nthe app and get a Curve card](http://curvecard.sjv.io/c/146261/1212032/12851), they'll give you £5 cashback through that\nlink.\n\n|     |     |\n| --- | --- |\n|  | [![Anker Powerrbank](https://www.seat61.com/images2/anker-powerbank.jpg)](https://www.amazon.co.uk/gp/search?ie=UTF8&keywords=Anker+powerbank&tag=themaninseats-21) |\n\n### Carry an Anker powerbank\n\nTickets, reservations, hotel bookings and Interrail or Eurail passes are often\nnow\nheld on your mobile phone.  You daren't let it run out of power, and you\ncan't always rely on the phone's internal battery or on being near a power\noutlet.  I always carry an\n[Anker \\\\\npowerbank](https://www.amazon.co.uk/gp/search?ie=UTF8&keywords=Anker+powerbank&tag=themaninseats-21) which can recharge my phone several times over.\n[Buy from Amazon.co.uk](https://www.amazon.co.uk/gp/search?ie=UTF8&keywords=Anker+powerbank&tag=themaninseats-21) or\n[buy from Amazon.com](https://www.amazon.com/s?k=Anker+powerbank&).\n\n### Touring cities?  Use hill walking shoes!\n\nOne of the best things I've done\nis swap my normal shoes for hill-walking shoes, in my case from Scarpa.\nThey're intended for hiking across the Pennines not wandering around Florence,\nbut the support and cushioning for hiking works equally well when you're on your\nfeet all day exploring foreign cities.  My feet used to give out first and\nlimit my day, now the rest of me gives up before they do!\n\n* * *\n\n[Back\\\\\nto 'Rail travel to Europe'\\\\\ngeneral page](https://www.seat61.com/european-train-travel.htm)\n\n[Back to home \\\\\npage](https://www.seat61.com/index.html)\n\n- Train travel UK & Ireland\n  -
